1-3 fixes: material a recipe field (form_pass0 version 5); bind gives an item named by a measurement unit and its parent to the measurement; measurement activity rows carry the method in facts.csv (experiments.csv holds no activity rows); 5 gao15 rows of binding_regression.csv updated (unit, how, reason), the other 793 unchanged.
4: gre23 has one specimen per run carrying all four measurements (sem_eds, xrd, photograph, two_point_through_thickness), no pass-0 measurement unit and no piece entity (the gold: four pieces per pair); mat22 pass 0 and the extension list 45 runs and no specimen, so none of the 54 gold specimens pairs.
5: material refill 3 calls (/bin/bash.05): 56 of 57 runs with material; gre23 run_120_160 zno set to null (its quote not found in abs.g3).
6: pass 1 whole paper under P184, 35 calls (/bin/bash.73); gre23 s9.p4 as expected (hold_A, hold_B, reactant_A, reactant_B, purge_A, purge_B); gao15 18 records about the 15 new units; mat22 grid runs: no flow or cycle number on a grid unit (no specimen exists); stat / between / n without an uncertainty 0, stage on pulse / purge 0. Adoption by the whole-paper rule: none (gao15 activity 14 -> 11, caveat 1 -> 0, observation 26 -> 22; gre23 caveat 7 -> 6, derived 21 -> 14, observation 26 -> 24; mat22 claim 27 -> 23, comparison 2 -> 0, setting 68 -> 62); form_versions.csv P184 rejected for the three; the old lines stay in use.
7 scores vs P184: no type drops; gao15 setting 29 -> 35, observation 23 -> 26, mat22 setting 67 -> 68; T6 2170 -> 2170. (A first compile dropped pass-1 material records about runs with a recipe material, my own choice: 9 T6 rows lost; reverted, records kept.)
7 checks: binding_regression 798 / 798; audits 1-5 PASS; export --check 19 / 19; cards 0 errors.
7 units: gao15 8 runs / 13 specimens / 20 measurements (gold 9 / 14; 3 gold units missing); gre23 4 / 4 / 28 (20 gold units missing); mat22 45 / 42 / 101 (69 gold units missing); runs without material 0; measurements without a method 38, all compile-derived; mat22 grid films: 0 own, 49 inherited, not inherited cycle_number, film_thickness, gas_flow_rate[ar], plasma_gas_ratio.
